PS C:\Users\astan\Desktop\llm-isabelle-main>
PS C:\Users\astan\Desktop\llm-isabelle-main>
(.venv) foreach ($ds in $datasets) {
  $file = $ds["file"]
  $depth = $ds["depth"]
  $timeout = $ds["timeout"]

  if (Test-Path $file) {
    Write-Host "`n=== OPENAI NO-SLEDGE | $file | $model ===" -ForegroundColor Cyan

    python -m prover.experiments bench --file $file --beam 3 --max-depth $depth --timeout $timeout --reranker both --model $model --shuffle --seed 42

    python -m prover.experiments aggregate --dir datasets\results --min-rows 1

    Write-Host "`n=== OPENAI SLEDGE | $file | $model ===" -ForegroundColor Green

    python -m prover.experiments bench --file $file --beam 3 --max-depth $depth --timeout $timeout --reranker both --sledge --quickcheck --nitpick --model $model --shuffle --seed 42

    python -m prover.experiments aggregate --dir datasets\results --min-rows 1
  }
  else {
    Write-Host "Skipping missing dataset: $file" -ForegroundColor Yellow
  }
}

>>   $bstr = [System.Runtime.InteropServices.Marshal]::SecureStringToBSTR($secure)
>>   try {
>>     $plain = [System.Runtime.InteropServices.Marshal]::PtrToStringBSTR($bstr)
>>     [Environment]::SetEnvironmentVariable($Name, $plain, "Process")
>>   }
>>   finally {
>>     [System.Runtime.InteropServices.Marshal]::ZeroFreeBSTR($bstr)
>>   }
>> }
